# Third-party notices

Task3B2 state-region, shallow/deep history and pseudostate semantics additionally translate the exact authorized MIT1.2026.9 CommandConcurrentState, Stereogroup, GroupMakerState, ConcurrentStates and three history/synchronization image primitives listed in provenance. Copyright 2009-2024 Arnaud Roques, Original Author Arnaud Roques; EntityImageDeepHistory additionally retains Contribution: Serge Wenger. Existing StateDiagram/link contributor credits and the complete retained MIT grant below remain. Region layout uses checked native Dagre and measured composition with visible dashed separators; container routes and shape-border intersections are original bounded native geometry. No Graphviz/Smetana/ELK builder implementation or assets are imported; singleton/named identity and explicit edges do not imply runtime state-machine execution or pixel parity.

Directional flow tile/port/cursor semantics and colors are translated from PlantUML MIT 1.2026.9 FlowDiagram/ActivityBox and Golem TilesField/Position/TileGeometry, copyright 2009–2024 Arnaud Roques; HColors also retains copyright2009–2025 Arnaud Roques. Exact hashes, credits, native geometry differences and destinations are in provenance/plantuml-mit-1.2026.9.json; complete MIT grant retained in provenance/LICENSE-PlantUML-MIT.txt. No Java grid-shift loop or external graph renderer is used.

Legacy BPM syntax, flow identities and start/event/merge semantics are translated from PlantUML MIT 1.2026.9, copyright 2009–2024 Arnaud Roques. Exact source/author lines, hashes, destinations and native geometry differences are recorded in provenance/plantuml-mit-1.2026.9.json; the complete MIT grant remains in provenance/LICENSE-PlantUML-MIT.txt. Native bounded branch composition replaces the original unchecked grid-cleaning loops.

Native EBNF/regex railroad syntax and ETile composition include translated PlantUML MIT 1.2026.9 semantics, copyright 2009–2024 and 2009–2020 Arnaud Roques. Exact paths, copyright/author lines and hashes are recorded in provenance/plantuml-mit-1.2026.9.json; the complete MIT grant is retained in provenance/LICENSE-PlantUML-MIT.txt. Native orthogonal rail geometry differs from PlantUML curves; no matching engine or external renderer is used.

## PlantUML parser generation

The engine directly selects the already locked `lalrpop-util =0.23.2` with default features disabled; its existing MIT/Apache notices below still apply. The separate developer-only `tools/plantuml-grammar-gen` crate selects `lalrpop =0.23.2` with default features disabled. Its 67-package registry checksum, exact unpacked-source, license-expression and selected-feature inventory is [provenance/plantuml-generator-dependencies.json](provenance/plantuml-generator-dependencies.json). Full selected grants and copyright notices are covered by the [primary generator notices](provenance/LICENSE-PlantUML-Generator-Dependencies.md), the [two-package supplement](provenance/LICENSE-PlantUML-Generator-Supplement.md), and reused existing notices in this file. The explicit [67-package coverage mapping](provenance/plantuml-generator-notice-coverage.json) verifies each exact package/version label, complete retained grant hash and selected license combination, including both required unicode-ident grants. This generator is outside the engine/viewer dependency graph and is never invoked by an ordinary build or render. A test-only `merman-core` feature supplies deterministic cancellation checkpoints without altering the shipped closure or audited vendor files.

The borrowed token, admission, error infrastructure and supported-subset definition grammar are original native code, separately inventoried in [provenance/plantuml-parser.json](provenance/plantuml-parser.json). Existing PlantUML literal evaluation semantics and their actual MIT credits remain in the semantic translation manifest; these infrastructure files do not acquire invented Java credits. Checked-in generated output retains the generator's mechanical version/hash header and is never hand-edited.

## Adapted pgui SVG font policy

`viewer/src/svg_fonts.rs` adapts the installed-font selection, missing generic-family repair, Unicode Emoji_Presentation test and platform emoji fallback order from `crates/gpui/src/svg_renderer.rs` in [Warsaw-Dynamics/pgui](https://github.com/Warsaw-Dynamics/pgui) at exact revision `f5d5fa2f8f07b0be68442e694d22ed6064609e73`. Original file SHA-256: `fb12dae87c0cd4c5f5a1fbbd2f9548d52fde9440c21e3d576dfeb0233eda88a6`. Copyright 2022 - 2025 Zed Industries, Inc.; Apache License 2.0. The full original copyright/grant and Apache licence text are retained in the gpui dependency section below and apply to this adapted source too.

Changes: specialize the original asset policy to the standalone viewer's empty asset source (no bundled fonts), share the system font database directly, and extract the resolver for the private same-tree display adapter. The adapter uses pgui's public `swap_rgba_pa_to_bgra` conversion and `RenderImage` API; no font files or upstream text-layout implementation were copied. Direct exact usvg0.46.0, resvg0.46.0, regex1.13.1, ttf-parser0.25.1 and svgtypes0.16.1 dependencies reuse the already licensed locked closure; no package/version is added.

## Native PlantUML translation

Task3N wire block/column/hierarchy, scoped identity and normal-link semantics derive from selected authorized MIT1.2026.9 wire sources, Copyright 2009-2024 Arnaud Roques, Original Author: Arnaud Roques. Exact source hashes, verbatim credits and Rust destinations are recorded in the same provenance manifest under the retained MIT grant. Native placement measures captions and gaps once, enforces containment for forced parents and paints vertical labels; bus/cursor/percent-anchor/spot/print semantics are not imported or admitted.

Task3L categorical chart axis/series/legend semantics derive from selected authorized MIT1.2026.9 chart sources, Copyright 2009-2024 Arnaud Roques, Original Author: David Fyfe. Exact selected source hashes, verbatim credits and Rust destinations are recorded in the PlantUML provenance manifest under the retained MIT grant. The native measured geometry, horizontal Y-axis title and strict sample-count/range admission deliberately differ from the original renderer; no chart assets or external runtime are imported.

Task3J additionally translates fourteen exact selected MIT Chen/description/symbol/link/color source files listed with hashes and destinations in the same manifest. All retain `(C) Copyright 2009-2024, Arnaud Roques` and `Original Author:  Arnaud Roques`; LinkType additionally retains `Contribution :  Hisashi Miyashita`. The complete MIT grant linked below applies, including the unchanged historical headers beneath the generated grant. Chen lexical ownership, separate node kinds and undirected cardinalities, and icon-free ArchiMate description rectangles derive from those sources. Only the two named color constants LightBlue=ADD8E6 and LightGreen=90EE90 are selected from ColorTrieNode; no broader color table/trie implementation or asset is imported. Checked Dagre adaptation, real boundary intersections, ellipse fitting, budgets and SVG glue are original native code. Weak/identifying/key/multi/derived, subtype and all icon semantics reject explicitly. Two base fixtures preserve exact original-reference bytes and two viewer examples are independently authored. No Smetana/Graphviz/stdlib/icon/font/theme asset or dependency is added; original pixels are not claimed.

Task3I additionally translates the selected MIT files/packetdiag/board/HCL grammar and semantics in the same provenance manifest. Files, board and HCL credit Copyright 2009-2024 Arnaud Roques. Packet sources retain both generated Copyright 2009-2024 and historical Copyright 2009-2026 Arnaud Roques lines; their Original Author is kolulu23, except CommandSameHeight credits Duy Nguyen. PacketDiagram, CommandNumRange and CommandScaleDirection additionally credit Contribution: The-Lum. Ordered HCL duplicate fields reuse minimal-json semantics, Copyright (c) 2016 EclipseSource, under the complete retained MIT grant. Native file/folder/note symbols use original geometry without Twemoji assets. Packet width-conservation, safe row clearance, full-label common scale and single board-root painting deliberately differ from upstream. Four exact original reference inputs are copied byte-for-byte and four additional viewer sources are independently authored native validation examples; no original pixel-parity claim.

The native sequence/class/object/description/state/activity/mindmap/WBS/JSON/YAML checkpoint derives its grammar and behavioral subset exclusively from the authorized PlantUML MIT edition 1.2026.9 source artifact. Copyright 2009-2024 Arnaud Roques; selected declaration/link files additionally credit contributions by Hisashi Miyashita. StateDiagram, CommandLinkStateCommon, CommandLinkState and CommandLinkStateReverse additionally credit Serge Wenger. Ordered JSON semantics additionally derive from embedded minimal-json, Copyright (c) 2016 EclipseSource, under MIT. The complete original generated MIT grant, distribution notice and embedded minimal-json MIT grant are retained in [provenance/LICENSE-PlantUML-MIT.txt](provenance/LICENSE-PlantUML-MIT.txt), incorporated here by reference. Exact unmodified source-file hashes, Rust destinations, translated behavior and verbatim contributor credit lines are recorded in [provenance/plantuml-mit-1.2026.9.json](provenance/plantuml-mit-1.2026.9.json).

Some authorized generated MIT-edition files retain historical upstream GPL comment headers beneath the distribution's MIT grant; the manifest preserves their exact bytes and edition rather than inventing a cleaned history. No other edition, rejected candidate port, Graphviz/Smetana/ELK implementation or theme/icon/sprite/font asset was imported from PlantUML. Native measured geometry, resource-budget glue and SVG writer are original implementations; WBS fork/trunk topology is traced to the authorized WBS sources. Existing audited Dugong/Merman notices below continue to apply; their direct use adds no new crate version to the locked dependency closure. The eleven PlantUML fixtures were freshly authored for this task and were independently admitted by the original MIT renderer, not copied from upstream or candidate examples.

The standalone diagram workspace includes the Rust crates below. Each selected licence text follows with its crates. Embedded translated-source and fixture notices follow in the second part.

The shared style/text checkpoint additionally translates the selected MIT document command, skinparam migration, plain selector precedence, Creole and data-prefix extraction semantics listed in the same manifest. All newly selected files credit `(C) Copyright 2009-2024, Arnaud Roques` and `Original Author:  Arnaud Roques`; the complete retained MIT grant above applies. The two `styledsequence.puml` and `styledgraph.puml` fixtures are independently authored native validation examples, not imported assets or original-renderer geometry oracles. No stylesheet, font, theme, color table, icon or resource-loading implementation is imported.

The bounded literal-constant checkpoint additionally translates only the scalar eager-freeze, original-input cursor and token-boundary portions of MIT `tim/EaterAffectationDefine.java`, `tim/TContext.java`, `tim/VariableManager.java`, and the previously selected `text/TLineType.java`. Each credits `(C) Copyright 2009-2024, Arnaud Roques` and `Original Author:  Arnaud Roques`; the complete MIT grant above applies. Exact file hashes, line anchors, exclusions and native restrictions are in the manifest. Immutable names, resource/origin accounting and compact source mapping are original native policy/glue. `constantsequence.puml` and `constantgraph.puml` are independently authored native examples, not original-renderer geometry oracles. No full TIM engine, function implementation, resource service or assets were imported.

Rust dependency section generated with `cargo about generate --config ../about.toml ../tools/third-party-notices.hbs --offline --locked` from the standalone Cargo.lock. Regenerate after dependency changes.

The bounded timing checkpoint additionally translates the eighteen exact MIT `timingdiagram` grammar/model/layout sources in the manifest. Their actual credits are `(C) Copyright 2009-2024, Arnaud Roques` and `Original Author:  Arnaud Roques`; the full MIT grant linked above applies. Integer timing, initial-state/equal-time ownership and constraint/message resolution are native subsets. Measured waveform/ribbon geometry, sparse tick selection and routing are original native implementations. `timingbase.puml`, `timingdual.puml` and `timinglane.puml` preserve independently authored reference-input bytes and hashes; `timing.puml` is an independently authored native viewer example. No source renderer, icon, font, theme or additional dependency was imported.

The bounded Gantt checkpoint additionally translates the seventeen exact MIT `gantt` task/project/duration/reference/date/solver/default-calendar/bar/diamond sources in the manifest. They credit `(C) Copyright 2009-2024, Arnaud Roques` and `Original Author:  Arnaud Roques`; the complete MIT grant linked above applies. ISO civil arithmetic, sparse measured date ticks and orthogonal routes are original native implementations. Freeze-after-reference, explicit predecessor duration and cycle rejection are narrower native admission policies. `ganttbase.puml`, `ganttalias.puml` and `ganttleap.puml` preserve exact independently authored original-reference inputs; `gantt.puml` is a new native viewer example. No renderer/calendar asset/theme/icon/font/additional dependency was imported.

## Licences

The bounded nwdiag checkpoint translates the ten exact MIT `nwdiag` command/model sources listed in the manifest. All credit `(C) Copyright 2009-2024, Arnaud Roques` and `Original Author:  Arnaud Roques`; their full MIT grant remains in [provenance/LICENSE-PlantUML-MIT.txt](provenance/LICENSE-PlantUML-MIT.txt). Translated semantics cover global server identity, separate interface memberships, network address/display, repeat-address updates, group ownership/header policy and painted properties. Strict whole-input admission, immutable typed models, sparse bus/column layout and foreign-crossing gaps are original native glue, with explicit narrower grammar and color/shape/hidden-network restrictions. `nwdiagbase.puml`, `nwdiagshared.puml` and `nwdiagrepeated.puml` preserve exact independently authored reference inputs; `nwdiag.puml` is an authored native viewer example. No upstream renderer/grid/theme/icon/font/asset, external layout code or dependency was imported; original geometry/pixel parity is not claimed.

The bounded Salt checkpoint translates the twenty-six exact MIT `salt` token/widget/table/size/control sources listed in the manifest, including rejection-only marker discovery for unimplemented tree/tab/menu/dropdown/scroll/line/region/image/dictionary constructs. Each credits `(C) Copyright 2009-2024, Arnaud Roques` and `Original Author:  Arnaud Roques`; the complete MIT grant in [provenance/LICENSE-PlantUML-MIT.txt](provenance/LICENSE-PlantUML-MIT.txt) applies. Typed escape tokens, sparse bottom-up constraint solving, immutable ownership and bounded scene glue are original native work. Plain field/button padding retains the original eight-unit character minimum; decoded visible longest-line counting is an explicit native formatted-text deviation. `saltbase.puml`, `saltwidgets.puml` and `saltliteral.puml` preserve exact independently authored original-reference inputs; `salt.puml` is an authored native viewer example. No image/dictionary/font/theme asset, resource loader or dependency was imported.

```
/// The PUA code points special-cased in the GB18030 encoder.
pub(crate) static GB18030_2022_OVERRIDE_PUA: [u16; 18] = [
    0xE78D, 0xE78E, 0xE78F, 0xE790, 0xE791, 0xE792, 0xE793, 0xE794, 0xE795, 0xE796, 0xE81E, 0xE826,
    0xE82B, 0xE82C, 0xE832, 0xE843, 0xE854, 0xE864,
];

/// The bytes corresponding to the PUA code points special-cased in the GB18030 encoder.
pub(crate) static GB18030_2022_OVERRIDE_BYTES: [[u8; 2]; 18] = [
    [0xA6, 0xD9],
    [0xA6, 0xDA],
    [0xA6, 0xDB],
    [0xA6, 0xDC],
    [0xA6, 0xDD],
    [0xA6, 0xDE],
    [0xA6, 0xDF],
```

```
#![allow(clippy::all)]
use crate::_alloc_prelude::*;
// Copied from regex_syntax crate to avoid pulling in the whole crate just for a utility function
// https://github.com/rust-lang/regex/blob/431c4e4867e1eb33eb39b23ed47c9934b2672f8f/regex-syntax/src/lib.rs
//
// Copyright (c) 2014 The Rust Project Developers
//
// Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any
// person obtaining a copy of this software and associated
// documentation files (the "Software"), to deal in the
// Software without restriction, including without
// limitation the rights to use, copy, modify, merge,
// publish, distribute, sublicense, and/or sell copies of
// the Software, and to permit persons to whom the Software
// is furnished to do so, subject to the following
// conditions:
//
// The above copyright notice and this permission notice
// shall be included in all copies or substantial portions
// of the Software.
//
// THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS", WITHOUT WARRANTY OF
// ANY KIND, EXPRESS OR IMPLIED, INCLUDING BUT NOT LIMITED
// TO THE WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY, FITNESS FOR A
// PARTICULAR PURPOSE AND NONINFRINGEMENT. IN NO EVENT
// SHALL THE AUTHORS OR COPYRIGHT HOLDERS BE LIABLE FOR ANY
// CLAIM, DAMAGES OR OTHER LIABILITY, WHETHER IN AN ACTION
// OF CONTRACT, TORT OR OTHERWISE, ARISING FROM, OUT OF OR
// IN CONNECTION WITH THE SOFTWARE OR THE USE OR OTHER
// DEALINGS IN THE SOFTWARE.

pub fn escape(text: &str) -> String {
    let mut quoted = String::new();
    escape_into(text, &mut quoted);
    quoted
}

fn escape_into(text: &str, buf: &mut String) {
    buf.reserve(text.len());
    for c in text.chars() {
        if is_meta_character(c) {
            buf.push('\\');
        }
        buf.push(c);
    }
}

fn is_meta_character(c: char) -> bool {
    match c {
        '\\' | '.' | '+' | '*' | '?' | '(' | ')' | '|' | '[' | ']' | '{' | '}' | '^' | '$'
        | '#' | '&' | '-' | '~' => true,
        _ => false,
    }
}

```

```
  typedef struct  BDF_encoding_el_
  {
    FT_ULong   enc;
    FT_UShort  glyph;

  } BDF_encoding_el;


  typedef struct  BDF_FaceRec_
  {
```

# Embedded translated sources and data

The engine wraps Merman and includes narrowly modified copies of the published 0.9.2 core/render crates. Original archive checksums, per-file import hashes and the explicit four-file patch ledger are retained under `vendor/`; original MIT/Apache license texts and all embedded-source notices/legal texts remain in each imported crate. Repairs are authored against MIT Mermaid revision `6d8921f94f9a12b76586eddcf45a566080d4767e` (MIT, Knut Sveidqvist 2014–2022). Its selected compiled packages include the following source/data notices, retained from their published archives. ELK packages and math font data are not part of the selected graph. Fixture adaptations separately use the same Mermaid documentation revision.

## Published source manifest: merman-core-0.9.2

## Third-Party Notices

This file records source translated, copied, generated, or embedded in `crates/merman-core`.
It is generated from `docs/release/THIRD_PARTY_COMPONENTS.json`.

### DOMPurify

Merman selects DOMPurify's Apache-2.0 option for generated sanitizer defaults; the exact upstream Apache-2.0 license file is preserved.

- Version: `3.4.16`
- Source: https://github.com/cure53/DOMPurify.git @ `b9b9d80f7e401771c2ccaef5f45def7eec8f27d7`

- License: `(Apache-2.0 OR MPL-2.0)`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/dompurify/LICENSE`

### Mermaid

Merman independently implements Mermaid-compatible behavior while translating selected algorithms, generating defaults, copying architecture icon data, and retaining upstream fixtures and snapshots.

- Version: `12.1.0`
- Source: https://github.com/mermaid-js/mermaid.git @ `21f72f07ea22c0af48a3149c550654e80d8e40cb`
- Relationship: `behavior-reference`, `copied`, `fixtures`, `generated`, `modified`, `translated`
- License: `MIT`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/mermaid/LICENSE`

Merman's URL sanitization behavior is a source-backed Rust translation of sanitize-url.

- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/sanitize-url/LICENSE`

### ZenUML Core

Merman's ZenUML grammar, model, renderer, emoji/icon data, and behavior probes follow the admitted ZenUML Core 3.50.1 source baseline.

- Source: https://github.com/iVis-at-Bilkent/layout-base.git @ `836898aa4a88e2794774997d7128b383108a3d5a`
- Relationship: `modified`, `translated`
- License: `MIT`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/layout-base-v1/LICENSE`

### layout-base 2.x

Manatee also follows the newer layout-base behavior selected by the FCoSE dependency graph.

- Version: `2.0.1`
- Source: https://github.com/iVis-at-Bilkent/layout-base.git @ `3f7549940feef31416cc35ef8256282ebc4d1ecd`
- Relationship: `modified`, `translated`
- License: `MIT`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/layout-base-v2/LICENSE`

## Published source manifest: merman-unicode-linebreak-0.9.2

## Third-Party Notices

This file records source translated, copied, generated, or embedded in `crates/merman-unicode-linebreak`.
It is generated from `docs/release/THIRD_PARTY_COMPONENTS.json`.

### unicode-linebreak

merman-unicode-linebreak is an Apache-2.0 fork of unicode-linebreak 0.1.5, preserving its published Unicode 15.0 generated tables and extending character iteration for cooperative cancellation. UPSTREAM.json records the exact v0.1.5 Git revision and crates.io archive SHA-256 3b09c83c3c29d37506a3e260c08c03743a6bb66a9cd432c6934ab501a190571f.

- Version: `0.1.5`
- Source: https://github.com/axelf4/unicode-linebreak @ `829adeed3c07ed160c1c721b61806eb902a4c1f0`
- Relationship: `copied`, `modified`
- License: `Apache-2.0`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/unicode-linebreak/LICENSE`
